from communication import Communication 
from database import Database 
import json 
import threading 
from rsa import RSA 
import bcrypt 
import time 
 
class Server(Communication): 
    def __init__(self, ip, port): 
        super().__init__(ip, port, is_server=True)  
        self.db = Database()  
        self.rsa = RSA()  
 
        self.ip_requests = {}  # אוגרים את הזמן של כל בקשה 
        self.failed_logins = {}  # אוגרים את מספר הכשלונות  
        self.blocked_ips = {}  # אוגרים את ה-IPים החסומים  
        self.request_limit = 50  
        self.time_window = 10  
        self.failed_attempts_limit = 10  # מספר כשלונות  מותר לפני חסימה  
        self.block_duration = 3600 # משך הזמן שכתובת  הIp  חסומה 
 
    def send_response(self, client_socket, response): 
        client_socket.sendall((json.dumps(response) + "__END__").encode('utf -8'))  
 
    def check_ddos_protection(self, client_ip: str): 
        if client_ip in self.blocked_ips:  
            block_time = self.blocked_ips[client_ip]  
            if time.time() - block_time < self.block_duration:  
                return False  # ה-IP עדיין חסום 
            else:  
                del self.blocked_ips[client_ip]  # שחרור ה -IP מהחסימה אם עבר הזמן  
 
        current_time = time.time()  
        if client_ip in self.ip_requests:  
            requests = self.ip_requests[client_ip]  
            self.ip_requests[client_ip] = [timestamp for timestamp in requests if  
Easy order  2025   רועי אביב  
Page | 111 
                                            current_time - timestamp < self.time_window]  
            if len(self.ip_requests[client_ip]) >= self.request_limit:  
                return False  # עבר את המגבלה  
        else:  
            self.ip_requests[client_ip] = []  
 
        # מוסיף את הזמן הנוכחי למילון  
        self.ip_requests[client_ip].append(current_time)  
        return True  
 
    def handle_failed_login(self, client_ip: str): 
        # אם ה-IP לא נמצא בכשלונות ,יתחיל  עם 0 
        if client_ip not in self.failed_logins:  
            self.failed_logins[client_ip] = 0  
 
        self.failed_logins[client_ip] += 1  
 
        if self.failed_logins[client_ip] >= self.failed_attempts_limit:  
            self.blocked_ips[client_ip] = time.time()  # חוסם את ה-IP 
            print(f"Blocked IP: {client_ip} due to too many failed login attempts")  
 
    def hash_password(self, password: str) -> str:  
        salt = bcrypt.gensalt()  
        hashed = bcrypt.hashpw(password.encode('utf -8'), salt)  
        return hashed.decode('utf -8')  
 
    def verify_password(self, password: str, hashed: str) -> bool:  
        return bcrypt.checkpw(password.encode('utf -8'), hashed.encode('utf -8'))  
 
    def start_server(self): 
        print("Server is listening for connections...")  
        while True:  
            client_socket, addr = self.accept_connection()  
            if client_socket:  
                # יצירת  Thread חדש לכל לקוח  
                threading.Thread(target=self.handle_client, args=(client_socket,)).start ()  
 
    def handle_client(self, client_socket): 
        client_ip = client_socket.getpeername()[0]  # שולפים את כתובת  ה-IP של הלקוח  
 
         #בדיקת  הגנה על  DDoS לפני כל בקשה  
        if not self.check_ddos_protection(client_ip):  
            response = {  
                "status": "error",  
                "message": "Too many requests. Please try again later."  
            }  
            self.send_response(client_socket, response)  
            client_socket.close()  
            return

 Database: 
import sqlite3 
from datetime import datetime, timedelta 
 
class Database: 
    def __init__(self, db_name=r"C: \Users \aviva \Dropbox \ מסמכים\רועי\ בית ספר\ כיתה 
יב\ סייבר \tabels.db"): 
        self.db_name = db_name  
        self.conn = None  
        self.cursor = None  
 
    def connect(self): 
        try:  
            self.conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_name, check_same_thread=False)  
            self.cursor = self.conn.cursor()  
            print("Connection to database established.")  
        except sqlite3.Error as e:  
            print(f"Error connecting to database: {e}")  
            return None  
        return self.cursor  
 
    def close(self): 
        if self.conn:  
            self.conn.clo se()  
            print("Connection closed.")  
 
    def authenticate_user(self, username): 
        query = "SELECT id, first_name, user_password, email_address FROM users WHERE 
user_name = ?" 
        self.cursor.execute(query, (username,))  
        result = self.cursor.fetchone()  
        return result if result else None  
 
    def register_user(self, first_name, last_name, email, phone, username, password): 
        self.cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM users WHERE user_name = ? OR email_address = ?", 
(username, em ail))  
        if self.cursor.fetchone():  
            return False  # Duplicate username or email  
        self.cursor.execute(  
            "INSERT INTO users (first_name, last_name, email_address, phone, user_name, 
user_password) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)", 
            (first_name, last_name, email, phone, username, password)  
        )  
        self.conn.commit()  
        return True  
 
    def find_tables(self, restaurant_id, table_size, category): 
        """ פונקציה שמחזירה את כל השולחנות המתאימים לפי מסעדה ,גודל שולחן  וקטגוריה""" 
        if category and category != "Not specified":

             table_query = """SELECT id, number_of_tables, category  
                             FROM tables  
                             WHERE restaurant_id = ?  
                             AND category = ?  
                             AND table_size >= ?"""  
            self.cursor.execute(table_query, (restaurant_id, category, table_size))  
        else:  
            # במקרה שאין קטגוריה - נבדוק את כל השולחנות המתאימים בגודל  
            table_query = """SELECT id, number_of_tables, category  
                             FROM tables  
                             WHERE restaurant_id = ?  
                             AND table_size >= ?"""  
            self.cursor.execute(table_query, (restaurant_id, table_size))  
 
        return self.cursor.fetchall()  
 
    def search_restaurants(self, table_size, time, date, food_types, districts, price, kosher, rating,

table_reservation_duration FROM restaurants WHERE 1=1"

            params.extend(districts)

            params.append(price)  
 
        if kosher == 1:  
            query += " AND kosher = ?"  
            params.append(1)  
 
        if rating:  
            query += " AND rating >= ?"  
            params.append(rating)  
 
        try:  
            self.cursor.execute(query, tuple(params))  
            restaurants = self.cursor.fetchall()  
            valid_restaurants = []

    def get_most_popular_restaurant(self): 
        query = """  
            SELECT r.name, COUNT(*) AS reservation_count  
            FROM reservations res  
            JOIN tables t ON res.table_id = t.id  
            JOIN restaurants r ON t.restaurant_id = r.id  
            GROUP BY r.id  
            ORDER BY reservation_count DESC  
            LIMIT 5  
        """  
        self.cursor.execute(query)  
        return self.cursor.fetchall()  
 
    def get_most_popular_users(self): 
        query = """  
            SELECT u.user_name, COUNT(r.id) as reservation_count  
            FROM users u  
            JOIN reservations r ON u.id = r.user_id  
            GROUP BY u.id  
            ORDER BY reservation_count DESC  
            LIMIT 5  
        """  
        self.cursor.execute(query)  
        return self.cursor.fetchall()

    def add_table(self, restaurant_name, category, table_size, number_of_tables): 
        try:  
            cursor = self.conn.cursor()  
 
            # שליפת  restaurant_id לפי שם המסעדה  
            cursor.execute("SELECT id FROM restaurants WHERE name = ?", (restaurant_name,))  
            result = cursor.fetchone()  
 
            if not result:  
                return False  # לא נמצאה מסעדה בשם הזה  
 
            restaurant_id = result[0]  
 
            # בדיקה אם כבר קיימת שורה עם אותם נתונים  
            cursor.execute("""  
                SELECT id, number_of_tables FROM tables  
                WHERE restaurant_id = ? AND category = ? AND table_size = ?  
            """, (restaurant_id, category, table_size))  
 
            existing_table = cursor.fetchone()  
 
            if existing_table:  
                # אם קיימת – עדכון כמות השולחנות  
                table_id, current_count = existing_table  
                new_count = current_count + number_of_tables  
                cursor.execute("""  
                    UPDATE tables SET number_of_tables = ?  
                    WHERE id = ?  
                """, (new_co unt, table_id))  
            else:  
                # אחרת – הכנסה חדשה  
                cursor.execute("""  
                    INSERT INTO tables (restaurant_id, category, table_size, number_of_tables)  
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)  
                """, (restaurant_id, category, table_size, number_of_tables))  
 
            self.conn.commit()  
            return True

    def remove_table(self, restaurant_name, category, table_size, number_to_remove): 
        try:  
            cursor = self.conn.cursor()  
 
            # שליפת  restaurant_id לפי שם המסעדה  
            cursor.execute("SELECT id FROM restaurants WHERE name = ?", (restaurant_name,))  
            result = cursor.fetchone()  
 
            if not result:  
                return False  # לא נמצאה מסעדה  
 
            restaurant_id = result[0]  
 
            # חיפוש השורה בטבלה לפי המסעדה ,קטגוריה  וגודל שולחן  
            cursor.execute("""  
                SELECT id, number_of_tables FROM tables  
                WHERE restaurant_id = ? AND category = ? AND table_size = ?  
            """, (restaurant_id, category, table_size))  
 
            table_row = cursor.fetchone()  
 
            if not table_row:  
                return False  # לא נמצאה שורה עם הפרטים האלו  
 
            table_id, current_count = table_row  
            new_count = current_count - number_to_remove  
 
            if new_coun t <= 0:  
                # מחיקה של השורה 
                cursor.execute("DELETE FROM tables WHERE id = ?", (table_id,))  
            else:  
                # עדכון הכמות  
                cursor.execute("UPDATE tables SET number_of_tables = ? WHERE id = ?", 
(new_count, table_id)) 
 
            self.conn.commit()  
            return True  
 
        except sqlite3.Error as e:  
            print("Database error in remove_table:", e)  
            return False

 Communication: 
import socket 
 
class Communication: 
    def __init__(self, ip, port, is_server=False): 
        self.ip = ip  
        self.port = port  
        self.is_server = is_server  
        self.socket = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)  
 
        if is_server:  
            try:  
                self.socket.bind((self.ip, self.port))  
                self.socket.listen(5)  
                print(f"Server listening on {self.ip}:{self.port}")  
            except Exception as e:  
                print(f"Server error : {e}")  
 
    def connect(self): 
        """  מתחבר לשרת כלקוח""" 
        if not self.is_server:  
            try:  
                self.socket.connect((self.ip, self.port))  
                print("Connected to server.")  
            except Exception as e:  
                print(f"Connection error: {e}")  
                self.socket = None  
 
    def accept_connection(self): 
        """ השרת מקבל חיבור חדש""" 
        if self.is_server:  
            try:  
                client_socket, addr = self.socket.accept()  
                print(f"New connection from {addr}")  
                return client_socket, addr  
            except Exception as e:  
                print(f"Error accepting connection: {e}")  
        return None, None  
 
    def send(self, message): 
        """ שליחת הודעה """ 
        if self.socket:  
            try:  
                if isinstance(message, str):  
                    message = message.encode('utf -8')  
                self.socket.sendall(message)  
            except Exception as e:  
                print(f"Error  sending message: {e}")  
                self.close()  
Easy order  2025   רועי אביב  
Page | 129 
  
    def receive(self): 
        """ קבלת הודעה מלאה  עם סימון סוף הודעה""" 
        if self.socket:  
            try:  
                data = b""  
                while True:  
                    part = self.socket.recv(1024)  
                    if not part:  
                        break  
                    data += part  
                    if b"__END__" in part:  
                        break  
                return data.decode('utf -8').replace("__END__", "")  
            except Exception as e:  
                print(f"Error receiving message: {e}")  
                self.close()  
        return None  
 
    def close(self): 
        """ סגירת חיבור""" 
        if self.socket:  
            try:  
                self.socket.close()  
                print("Connection closed.")  
            except Exception as e:  
                print(f"Error closing connection: {e}")  
            self.socket = None

 Rsa: 
import random 
 
class RSA: 
    def __init__(self, p=None, q=None): 
        if not p or not q:  
            p, q = self.generate_random_primes()  
 
        self.p = p  
        self.q = q  
        self.n = p * q  
        self.phi = (p - 1) * (q - 1)  
        self.public_key, self.private_key = self.generate_keypair()  
 
    def generate_random_primes(self): 
        prime_list = [53, 59, 61, 67, 71, 73, 79, 83, 89, 97, 101]  
        p = random.choice(prime_list)  
        q = random.choice(prime_list)  
        while q == p:  
            q = random.choice(prime_list)  
        return p, q  
 
    def gcd(self, a, b): 
        while b != 0:  
            a, b = b, a % b  
        return a  
 
    def multiplicative_inverse(self, e, phi): 
        d = 0  
        x1, x2 = 0, 1  
        y1 = 1  
        temp_phi = phi  
 
        while e > 0:  
            temp1 = temp_phi // e  
            temp2 = temp_phi - temp1 * e  
            temp_phi, e = e, temp2  
 
            x = x2 - temp1 * x1  
            y = d - temp1 * y1  
 
            x2, x1 = x1, x  
            d, y1 = y1, y  
 
        if temp_phi == 1:  
            return d + phi  
 
    def generate_keypair(self): 
        e = random.randrange(2, self.phi)  
Easy order  2025   רועי אביב  
Page | 131 
         while self.gcd(e, self.phi) != 1:  
            e = random.randrange(2, self.phi)  
        d = self.multiplicative_inverse(e, self.phi)  
        return ((e, self.n), (d, self.n))  
 
    def encrypt(self, plaintext, key): 
        e, n = key  
        byte_data = plaintext.encode('utf -8')  
        encrypted = [(byte ** e) % n for byte in byte_data]  
        return encrypted  
 
    def decrypt(self, ciphertext, key): 
        d, n = key  
        decrypted = [chr((byte ** d) % n) for byte in ciphertext]  
        retur n ''.join(decrypted)  
 
    def encrypt_as_str(self, plaintext, key): 
        encrypted_list = self.encrypt(plaintext, key)  
        return ','.join(map(str, encrypted_list))  
 
    def decrypt_from_str(self, encrypted_str, key): 
        encrypted_list = list(map(int, encrypted_str.split(',')))  
        return self.decrypt(encrypted_list, key)

import smtplib 
import random 
from email.mime.text import MIMEText  
from email.mime.multipart import MIMEMultipart 
 
 
class MailSender: 
    def __init__(self): 
        self.sender_email = "easy.ordermail1@gmail.com"  
        self.sender_password = "dxjv hmjt blwq getl"  
        self.smtp_server = "smtp.gmail.com"  
        self.smtp_port = 587  
        self.last_code_sent = None  
 
    def generate_verification_code(self): 
        self.last_code_sent = "".join([str(random.randint(0, 9)) for _ in range(8)])  
        return self.last_code_sent  
 
    def send_verification_code(self, recipient_email):  
        code = self.generate_verification_code()  
 
        message = MIMEMultipart()  
        message["From"] = self.sender_email  
        message["To"] = recipient_email  
        message["Subject"] = "Your Verification Code"  
 
        body = f"Hello! \n\nYour verification code is: {code} \n\nIf you did not request this, please 
ignore this email." 
        message.attach(MIMEText(body, "plain"))  
 
        try:  
            with smtplib.SMTP(self.smtp_server, self.smtp_po rt) as server:  
                server.starttls()  
                server.login(self.sender_email, self.sender_password)  
                server.sendmail(self.sender_email, recipient_email, message.as_string())  
            return code  # ← כאן מחזיר את הקוד עצמו  
        except Exception as e:  
            print("Failed to send email:", e)  
            return None

from communication import Communication 
from gui import GUI 
 
class Client(Communication): 
    def __init__(self, ip, port): 
        super().__init__(ip, port, is_server=False)  
        self.connect()  # מתחבר לשרת אחרי  יצירת  ה- Client 
        self.start_gui()  # מפעיל את ה- GUI 
 
    def start_gui(self): 
        # כאן נעביר  את הלקוח  ל- GUI 
        gui = GUI(self)  # מעביר את ה- Client  ל- GUI, כדי ש- GUI יוכל להשתמש ב- Communication של 
הלקוח  
        gui.run()  # מתחיל את  הלולאת ה- GUI

    def login(self): 
        username = self.username_entry.get().strip()  
        password = self.password_entry.get().strip()  
 
        if not username or not password:  
            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "Please fill in both username and password.")  
            return  
 
        if username == "admin" and password == "0000":  
            self.open_admin_panel()  
            return  
 
        encrypted_password = self.rsa.encrypt_as_str(password, self.server_public_key)  
 
        login_data = {  
            'username': username,  
            'encrypted_password': encrypted_password,  
            'action': 'login'  
        }  
        self.client_comm.send(json.dumps(login_data).encode('utf -8'))  
 
        response = self.client_comm.receive()  
        data = json.loads(response.encode('utf -8'))  
        check = data.get('status')  
 
        if check == "success":  
            self.username = username  
            self.user_id = data.get("user_id")  
            self.user_first_name = data.get("first_name")  
            self.user_email = data.get("email_address")  # ודא שהשרת מחזיר את האימייל  
 
             #פתיחת  חלון האימות מידית  
            self.open_verification_window()  
 
            # שליחת הקוד בת 'רד נפרד  
            threading.Thread(  
Easy order  2025   רועי אביב  
Page | 142 
                 target=self.send_code_thread,  
                daemon=True  
            ).start()  
        else:  
            messagebox.showerror("Login Error", data.get('message'))  
 
    def send_code_thread(self): 
        try:  
            self.mail_sender = MailSender()  
            self.verification_code = self.mail_sender.send_verification_code(self.user_email)  
            if not self.verification_code:  
                self.root.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Error",  
                                                                "Failed to send verification code. Please try again."))  
        except Exception as e:  
            self.root.after(0, lambda: messagebox.showerror("Error", f"Mail sending failed: \n{e}"))

    def resend_verification_code(self): 
        self.verification_code = self.mail_sender.send_verification_code(self.user_email)  
        self.remaining_seconds = 120  # איפוס טיימר  
        messagebox.showinfo("Code Resent", f"A new verification code was sent to 
{self.user_email}") 
        self.start_verification_timer()  
 
    def start_verification_timer(self): 
        if getattr(self, "timer_running", False):  #  בודק אם כבר רץ  
            return  
        self.timer_running = True  
        self.remaining_seconds = 120  
 
        def update_timer():  
            if self.remaining_seconds > 0:  
                mins, secs = divmod(self.remaining_seconds, 60)  
                timer_text = f"        Code expires in: {mins:02}:{secs:02}"  
 
                # שינוי צבע  לפי זמן 
                if self.remaining_seconds > 60:  
                    color = "green"  
                elif self.remaining_seconds > 30:  
                    color = "orange"  
                else:  
                    color = "red"  
 
                # עדכון תצוגה  
                self.timer_label.configure(text=timer_text, text_color=color, font=("Arial", 18, "bold"))  
                self.remaining_seconds -= 1  
                self.timer_label.after(1000, update_timer)  
            else:  
                self.timer_ label.configure(text="   Verification code expired", text_color="red",  
                                           font=("Arial", 18, "bold"))  
                self.verification_code = None  
                self.timer_running = False  
Easy order  2025   רועי אביב  
Page | 144 
  
        update_timer()  
 
    def verify_code(self): 
        entered_code = self.code_entry.get().strip()  
        if self.verification_code is None:  
            messagebox.showerror("Code Expired", "The verification code has expired. Please click 
'Send Again'.") 
            return  
 
        if entered_code == self.verification_code:  
            self.verification_window.destroy()  
            self.login_frame.pack_forget()  
            self.create_widgets()  
            messagebox.showinfo("Login Verified", f"Wel come back {self.user_first_name}!")  
        else:  
            messagebox.showerror("Verification Failed", "Incorrect verification code. Try again.")  
 
    def register(self): 
        # שלב ההרשמה  
        first_name = self.first_name_entry.get().strip()  
        last_name = self.last_name_entry.get().strip()  
        phone = self.phone_entry.get().strip()  
        email = self.email_entry.get().strip()  
        username = self.reg_username_entry.get().strip()  
        password = self.reg_password_entry.get().strip ()  
        confirm_password = self.reg_confirm_password_entry.get().strip()  
 
        if not (first_name and last_name and phone and email and username and password and 
confirm_password): 
            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "Please fill in all fields.")  
            return  
 
        if password != confirm_password:  
            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "Passwords do not match.")  
            return  
 
        if not self.is_valid_email(email):  
            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "Invalid email format.")  
            return  
 
        # בדיקת חוזק סיסמה  
        strength = self.evaluate_password_strength(password)  
if strength == "Weak": 
            messagebox.showerror("Password Strength",  
                                 "Your password is too weak. Use uppercase, lowercase, symbols and at least 8 
characters.") 
            return  
 
        # הצפנת הסיסמה בעזרת המפתח  הציבורי  
Easy order  2025   רועי אביב  
Page | 145 
         encrypted_password = self.rsa.encrypt_as_str(password, self.server_public_key)  
 
        # שליחת נתוני ההרשמה לשרת  
        register_data = {  
            'action': "register",  
            'first_name': first_name,  
            'last_name': last_name,  
            'phone': phone,  
            'email': email,  
            'username': username,  
            'encrypted_password': encrypted_password,  
        }  
 
        self.client_comm.send(json.dumps(register_data).encode('utf -8'))  
        response = self.clie nt_comm.receive()  
        data = json.loads(response.encode('utf -8'))  
        check = data.get('status')  
 
        if check == "success":  
            messagebox.showinfo("Registration Successful", "You have successfully registered!")  
 
            if self.register_frame:  
                self.register_frame.pack_forget()  
                self.register_frame = None  
 
            self.create_widgets()  
        else:  
            messagebox.showerror("Registration Error", data.get('message'))  
 
    # פונקציית עזר פנימית  במחלקה – אפשר להוציא אותה אם אתה מעדיף  
    def evaluate_password_strength(self, password): 
        import re  
        strength_points = 0  
        if len(password) >= 8:  
            strength_points += 1  
        if re.search(r"[A -Z]", password):  
            strength_points += 1  
        if re.search(r"[a -z]", password):  
            strength_points += 1  
        if re.search(r"[^A -Za-z0-9]", password):  # תווים מיוחדים  
            strength_points += 1  
 
        if strength_points == 4:  
            return "Stron g"  
        elif strength_points == 3 or len(password) >= 8:  
            return "Medium"  
        else:  
            return "Weak"  
 
 
Easy order  2025   רועי אביב  
Page | 146 
     def update_password_strength(self, event=None): 
        password = self.reg_password_entry.get()  
        strength = self.evaluate_password_strength(password)  
 
        if strength == "Strong":  
            color = "green"  
        elif strength == "Medium":  
            color = "orange"  
        else:  
            color = "red"  
 
        self.password_strength_label.configure(text=f"Password strength: {strength}", 
text_color=color) 
 
    def back_to_login_register_screen(self): 
        if self.login_frame:  
            self.login_frame.pack_forget()  
            self.login_frame = None  
 
        if self.register_frame:  
            self.register_frame.pack_forget()  
            self.register_frame = None  
 
        self.create_login_register_screen()  
 
    def is_valid_email(self, email): 
        # בדיקה אם כתובת  האימייל תקינה  
        return re.match(r"[^@]+@[^@]+ \.[^@]+", email)  
 
    def is_valid_date_format(self, date_str): 
        return re.fullmatch(r" \d{4} -\d{2} -\d{2}", date_str) is not None  
 
    def is_valid_time_format(self, time_str): 
        return re.fullmatch(r"([01] \d|2[0 -3]):[0 -5]\d", time_str) is not None  
 
    def is_date_time_valid(self, date_str, time_str): 
        """  בודק שהתאריך  לא עבר ,ואם היום – שהשעה מאוחרת יותר מהשעה הנוכחית""" 
 
        if not self.is_valid_date_format(date_str):  
            return False, "Invalid date format. Please enter in YYYY -MM -DD format."  
 
        if not self.is_valid_time_format(tim e_str):  
            return False, "Invalid time format. Please enter in HH:MM format."  
 
        # בדיקה שהתאריך באמת תקף  
        try:  
            user_date = datetime.strptime(date_str, "%Y -%m-%d").date()  
        except ValueError:  
            return False, "Invalid date. Please enter a real date."  
 
Easy order  2025   רועי אביב  
Page | 147 
         user_time = datetime.strptime(time_str, "%H:%M").time()  
 
        now = datetime.now()  
        today = now.date()  
        current_time = now.time()  
 
        if user_date < today:  
            return False, "The selected date has already passed. Please choose a future date."  
 
        if user_date == today and user_time <= current_time:  
            return False, "The selected time has already passed today. Please choose a later time."  
 
        return True, "Date and time are valid."

                except ValueError as e:  
                    print("  שגיאה בפירוש תאריך ,": e) 
                    return datetime.min

    def confirm_cancel_order(self, order): 
        answer = messagebox.askyesn o(  
            "Cancel Order",  
            f"Are you sure you want to cancel your reservation at {order['restaurant_name']} "  
            f"on {order['reservation_date']} at {order['reservation_time']}?"  
        )  
        if not answer:  
            return

Page | 153 
         cancel_request = {  
            "action": "cancel_reservation",  
            "reservation_id": order['reservation_id']  
        }  
        self.client_comm.send(json.dumps(cancel_request).encode('utf -8'))  
        response = self.client_comm.receive()  
        data = json.loads(response.encode('utf -8'))  
 
        if data.get("status") == "success":  
            threading.Thread(target=self.send_cancelled_reservation_thread, args=(order,), 
daemon=True).start() 
            messagebox.showinfo("Cancelled", "Re servation cancelled successfully. A confirmation 
email was sent.") 
            self.orders_frame.destroy()  
            self.show_user_orders()  
        else:  
            messagebox.showerror("Error", data.get("message"))  
 
    def send_cancelled_reservation_thread(self, order): 
        try:  
            if self.user_email and self.mail_sender:  
                self.mail_sender.send_cancel_confirmation(self.user_email, order)  
        except Exception as e:  
            print(f"Failed to send cancellation email: {e}")  
            messagebox.showwarning("Email Error",  
                                   "The reservation was cancelled, but we couldn't send the confirmation 
email.") 
 
    def search_restaurant(self): 
        if not self.client_comm.socket:  
            messagebox.showerror("Connection Error", "No connection to the server.")  
            return  
 
        num_people = self.people_entry.get().strip()  
        time = self.time_entry.get().strip()  
        average_pr ice_per_person = self.price_entry.get().strip()  
        date = self.date_entry.get().strip()  
 
        if not num_people or not time or not date:  
            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "You must enter the number of people, the time and 
the date.") 
            return  
 
        valid_date_time, date_time_message = self.is_date_time_valid(date, time)  
        if not valid_date_time:  
            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", date_time_message)  
            return  
        if not num_people.isdigit():  
            messagebox.showerror("Input Error", "The number of people must be a whole number.")  
            return  
Easy order  2025   רועי אביב  
Page | 154 
  
        food_type = self.food_listbox.get()  
        food_types = [] if food_type == "No Preference" else [food_type]  
 
        district = self.districts_listbox.get()  
        districts = [] if district == "No Preference" else [district]  
        kosher = self.kosher_var.get()  
        rating = self.rating_var.get()  
        seating = self.seating_var.get()  
 
        request_data = {  
            'action': 'search',  
            'table_size': num_people,  
            'time': time,  
            'date': date,  
            'food_types': food_types,  
            'districts': districts,  
            'price': average_price_per_person,  
            'kosher': kosher,  
            'rating': rating,  
            'category': seating  
        }  
        print("Sending request data:", request_data)  
        self.client_comm.send(json.dumps(request_data).encode('utf -8'))  
 
        # Receiving the response from the server  
        response = self.client_comm.receive()  
        if response:  
            try:  
                restaurants = json.loa ds(response)  
                print(restaurants)  
                if restaurants:  
                    self.show_restaurants(restaurants)  
                else:  
                    messagebox.showinfo("No Results", "No restaurants found matching your criteria.")  
            except Exception as e:  
                messagebox.showerror("JSON Error", f"Failed to decode the response: {e}")  
        else:  
            messagebox.showerror("Server Error", "Failed to get a response from the server.")

    def seating_category(self): 
        if self.seating_var.get() != "Not specified":  
            self.show_order_table_button()  
        else:  
            if self.available_seating == "Indoor and Outdoor":  
                self.show_seating_preference_window()  
            else:  
                if self.available_seating == "Indoor":  
                    self.seating_var.set("Indoor")  
                elif self.av ailable_seating == "Outdoor":  
                    self.seating_var.set("Outdoor")  
                self.show_order_table_button()  
 
    def show_seating_preference_window(self):

    def show_order_confirmation_window(self): 
        request_order = {  
            'action': 'order',  
            'restaurant': self.restaurant,  
            'category': self.seating_var.get(),  
            'table_size': self.people_entry.get(),  
            'rese rvation_time': self.time_entry.get(),  
            'date': self.date_entry.get(),  
            'user_id': self.user_id  
        }  
 
        self.client_comm.send(json.dumps(request_order).encode('utf -8'))  
        response = self.client_comm.receive()  
 
        if response:  
            try:  
                data = json.loads(response)  
                status = data.get("status")  
 
                if status == "success":  
                    raw_end_time = data.get("end_reservation")  
                    request_order[ "end_reservation"] = raw_end_time[:5] if raw_end_time else None  
                    threading.Thread(  
                        target=self.send_confirmed_reservation_thread,  
                        args=(request_order,),  
                        daemon=True  
                    ).start()  
 
                    confirmation_window = ctk.CTkToplevel(self.root)  
                    confirmation_window.title("Reservation Confirmed")  
                    confirmation_window.geometry("500x580")  
                    confi rmation_window.configure(fg_color="#f5f5f5")  
 
Easy order  2025   רועי אביב  
Page | 160 
                     confirmation_window.lift()  
                    confirmation_window.grab_set()  
                    confirmation_window.focus_force()  
 
                    #  לוגו 
                    try:  
                        logo_image = Image.open("logo.jpeg").resize((100, 100))  
                        logo_ctk_image = ctk.CTkImage(light_image=logo_image, size=(100, 100))  
                        ctk.CTkLabel(confirmation_window, text="", 
image=logo_ctk_image).pack(pady=(15, 10)) 
                    except Exception as e:  
                        print(" בעיה בטעינת  הלוגו ,": e) 
 
                    #  כותרת 
                    ctk.CTkLabel(  
                        confirmation_window,  
                        text="Reservation Confirmed!",  
                        font=ctk.CTkFont(size=20, weight="bold"),  
                        text_color="#333333"  
                    ).pack(pady=(0, 15))  
 
                    # מסגרת מידע  
                    info_frame = ctk.CTkFrame(confirmation_window, fg_color="#ffffff", 
corner_radius=10) 
                    info_frame.pack(padx=30, pady=10, fill="x")  
 
                    info_texts = [  
                        ("                     Restaurant:", self.restaurant),  
                        ("             Date:", self.date_entry.get()),  
                        ("        Time:", f"{self.time_entry.get()} - {request_order.get('end_reservation')}"),  
                        ("   People:", self.people_entry.get()),  
                        ("         Seating:", self.seating_var.get())  
                    ]  
 
                    for label, value in info_texts:  
                        row = ctk.CTkFrame(info_frame, fg_color="transparent")  
                        row.pack(anchor="w", pady=5, padx=10)  
                        ctk.CTkLabel(row, text=label, font=ctk.CTkFont(size=14, weight="bold"),  
                                     text_color="#444444").pack(side="left")  
                        ctk.CTkLabel(row, text=value, font=ctk.CTkFont(size=14), 
text_color="#666666").pack(side="left", 
                                                                                                            padx=10)  
 
                    #  טקסט אישור  
                    ctk.CTkLabel(  
                        confirmation_window,  
                        text="A confirmation email has been sent to your inbox.",  
                        font=ctk.CTkFont(size=13),  
                        text_color="#666666"  
Easy order  2025   רועי אביב  
Page | 161 
                     ).pack(pady=(15, 10))  
 
                    # כפתור סגירה  
                    ctk.CTkButton(  
                        confirmation_window,  
                        text="Close",  
                        command=self.close_application,  
                        font=ctk.CTkFont(size=16, weight="bold"),  
                        height=48,  
                        width=180,  
                        corner_radius=12,  
                        fg_color="#3B82F6",  
                        hover_color="#2 563EB",  
                        text_color="white"  
                    ).pack(pady=20)  
 
                else:  
                    messagebox.showinfo("Error", "There was an issue with your reservation.")  
            except json.JSONDecodeError:  
                messagebox.showinfo("Error", "Failed to decode response from server.")  
        else:  
            messagebox.showinfo("Error", "No response received from server.")  
 
    def send_confirmed_reservation_thread(self, order): 
        try:  
            if sel f.user_email and self.mail_sender:  
                self.mail_sender.send_reservation_confirmation(  
                    recipient_email=self.user_email,  
                    restaurant=order['restaurant'],  
                    city=order.get('city'),  # אם אין עיר – ברירת מחדל  
                    date=order['date'],  
                    start_time=order['reservation_time'],  
                    end_time = order['end_reservation'],  
                    people=order['table_size'],  
                    seating_catego ry=order['category']  
                )  
        except Exception as e:  
            print(f"Failed to send confirmation email: {e}")

Easy order  2025   רועי אביב  
Page | 164 
         content_frame.pack(padx=25, pady=10, fill="both", expand=True)  
 
        calendar = Calendar(content_frame, date_pattern="yyyy -mm-dd", selectmode="day")  
        calendar.pack(pady=25)  
 
        def on_date_confirm():  
            selected_date = calendar.get_date()  
            request_data = {  
                "action": "admin_view_orders_by_date",  
                "date": selected_date  
            }  
            self.client_comm.send(json.dumps(request_data).encode("utf -8"))  
            response = self.c lient_comm.receive()  
 
            if response:  
                try:  
                    response_data = json.loads(response)  
                    if response_data.get('status') == 'success':  
                        orders = response_data.get('orders', [])  
                        self.show_orders_result_window(orders, selected_date)  
                    else:  
                        messagebox.showerror("Error", "No orders on this date.")  
                except json.JSONDecodeError:  
                    message box.showerror("Error", "Failed to decode server response.")  
            else:  
                messagebox.showerror("Error", "No response from server.")  
 
        ctk.CTkButton(  
            date_window,  
            text="Show Orders",  
            command=on_date_confirm,  
            font=ctk.CTkFont(size=15),  
            fg_color="#2980b9", hover_color="#1f618d",  
            text_color="white", width=150, height=40, corner_radius=12  
        ).pack(pady=20)

    def view_popular_restaurants(self): 
        # שליחת בקשה לשרת  
        request_data = {  
            "action": "admin_view_popular_restaurants"  
        }  
        self.client_comm.send(json.dumps(request_data).encode("utf -8"))  
        response = self.client_comm.receive()  
 
        if response:  
            try:  
                response_data = json.loads(response)  
                if response_data.get("status") == "success":  
                    restaurants = response_data.get("restaurants", [])  
                    self.show_popular_restaurants_window(restaurants)  
                else:  
                    messagebox.showerror("Error",  
                                         response_data.get("message", "Failed to retrieve popular restaurants."))  
            except json.JSONDecodeError:  
                messagebox.showerror("Error", "Failed to decode server response.")  
        else:  
            messagebox.showerror("Error", "No response from serve r.")

    def view_active_users(self): 
        request_data = {  
            "action": "admin_view_active_users"  
        }  
        self.client_comm.send(json.dumps(request_data).encode("utf -8"))  
        response = self.client_comm.receive()  
 
        if response:  
            try:  
                response_data = json.loads(response)  
                if response_data.get('status') == 'success':  
                    users = response_data.get('users', [])  
                    self.show_active_users_result_window(users)  
                else:  
                    messagebox.showerror("Error", response_data.get("message", "Failed to retrieve 
active users.")) 
            except json.JSONDecodeError:  
                messagebox.showerror("Error", "Failed to decode server response.")  
        else:  
            messagebox.showerror("Error", "No response from server.")

     def add_restaurant(self): 
        add_window = ctk.CTkToplevel(self.root)  
        add_window.title("Add New Restaurant")  
        add_window.geometry("600x700")  
        add_window.configure(bg="#ecf0f1")  
 
        add_window.lift()  
        add_window.grab_set()

            ("Food Type", "food_type"),  
            ("City", "city"),  
            ("District", "district"),  
            ("Average Price Per Person", "average_price _per_person"),  
            ("Rating (1 -5)", "rating")  
        ]  
 
        #  יצירת 2  טורים

            ctk.CTkLabel(add_window, text=label_text).grid(row=row, column=0, padx=10, pady=5, 
sticky="w") 
            entry = ctk.CTkEntry(add_window, placeholder_text=label_text)  
            entry.grid(row=row, column=1, padx=10, pady=5, sticky="w")  
            entries[key] = entry  
            row += 1  
 
        # שדות זמן פתיחה וסגירה  
        ctk.CTkLabel(add_wind ow, text="Opening Time (HH:MM)").grid(row=row, column=0, 
padx=10, pady=5, sticky="w") 
        opening_time_entry = ctk.CTkEntry(add_window, placeholder_text="Opening Time")  
        opening_time_entry.grid(row=row, column=1, padx=10, pady=5, sticky="w")  
        row += 1  
 
        ctk.CTkLabel(add_window, text="Closing Time (HH:MM)").grid(row=row, column=0, 
padx=10, pady=5, sticky="w") 
        closing_time_entry = ctk.CTkEntry(add_window, placeholder_text="Closing Time")  
        closing_time_entry.grid(row=row, column=1, padx=10, pady=5, sticky="w")  
        row += 1  
 
        # זמן שליחת ההזמנה  
Easy order  2025   רועי אביב

         entries["opening_time"] = opening_time_entry  
        entries["closing_time"] = closing_time_entry  
 
        # שדה הזמנת משך השולחן

        table_reservation_duration_entry.grid(row=ro w, column=1, padx=10, pady=5, sticky="w")  
        entries["table_reservation_duration"] = table_reservation_duration_entry  
        row += 1  
 
        # RadioButtons לבחירת כשר או לא  
        kosher_var_a = ctk.StringVar(value="0")  
        kosher_label = ctk.CTkLabel(add_window, text="Kosher (1 = Yes, 0 = No)").grid(row=row,

        kosher_radio_no = ctk.CTkRadioButton(add_window, text="No", value="0", 
variable=kosher_var_a) 
        kosher_radio_yes.grid(row=row + 1, column=0, pady=5)  
        kosher_radio_no.grid(row=row + 1, column=1, pady=5)  
 
        def on_submit():  
            # קבלת הערכים מהטופס  
            try:  
                restaurant_data = {  
                    key: entry.get() for key, entry in entries.items()  
                }  
 
                # המרות טיפוסים לשדות שדורשים זאת  
                restaurant_data["table_reservation_duration"] = 
int(restaurant_data["table_reservation_duration"]) 
                restaurant_data["average_price_per_person"] = 
float(restaurant_data["average_price_per_person"]) 
                restaurant_data["kosher"] = int(kosher_var_a.get())  
                restaurant_data["rating"] = float(restaurant_data["rating"])  
 
                food_types = ["No Preference", "Italian", "Sushi", "Mea t", "Cafa", "Shawarma", 
"Hummus", "Burgers", 
                              "Mexican", "Fish", "Doner"]  
                districts = ["No Preference", "Gush Dan", "Sharon", "North", "Shfela", "Southeast", 
"Galil", "South"] 
                # בדיקת פורמט הזמן  
                if not self.is_valid_time_format(restaurant_data["opening_time"]):  
                    messagebox.showerror("Error", "Invalid opening time format. Use HH:MM (24 -hour 
format).")  
                    return  
Easy order  2025   רועי אביב  
Page | 175 
                 if not self.is_valid_time_format(restaurant_data["closing_time"]):  
                    messagebox.showerror("Error", "Invalid closing time format. Use HH:MM (24 -hour 
format).") 
                    return  
                if not restaurant_data["food_type"] in food_types:  
                    messagebox.showerror("Error", "food type does not exist")  
                    return  
                if not restaurant_data["district"] in districts:  
                    messagebox.showerror("Error", "dist rict does not exist")  
                    return  
 
            except ValueError:  
                messagebox.showerror("Error", "Invalid numeric input in one or more fields.")  
                return  
 
            # שליחת הבקשה לשרת 
            request_data = {  
                "action": "admin_add_restaurant",  
                "restaurant": restaurant_data  
            }  
 
            self.client_comm.send(json.dumps(request_data).encode("utf -8"))  
            response = self.client_comm.receive()  
 
            if response:  
                try:  
                    response_data = json.loads(response)  
                    if response_data.get("status") == "success":  
                        messagebox.showinfo("Success", "Restaurant added succ essfully.")  
                        add_window.destroy()  
                    else:  
                        messagebox.showerror("Error", response_data.get("message", "Failed to add 
restaurant.")) 
                except json.JSONDecodeError:  
                    messagebox.showerror("Error", "Failed to decode server response.")  
            else:  
                messagebox.showerror("Error", "No response from server.")  
 
        ctk.CTkButton(add_window, text="Add Restaurant", command=on_submit, 
font=ctk.CTkFon t(size=14),  
                      fg_color="#2ecc71", hover_color="#27ae60").grid(row=row + 2, column=0, 
columnspan=2, pady=20)

            restaurant_name = name_entry.get()  
            category = add_category_var.get()

                table_size = int(size_entry.get())  
                number_of_tables = int(count_entry.get())

            else:  
                messagebox.showerror("Error", "No response from server.")

    def delete_restaurant(self): 
        delete_window = ctk.CTkToplevel(self.root)  
        delete_window.title("Delete Restaurant")  
Easy order  2025   רועי אביב  
Page | 180 
         delete_window.geometry("400x300")  
        delete_window.configure(bg="#ecf0f1")  
 
        delete_window.lift()  
        delete_window.grab_set()  
        delete_window.focus_force()  
 
        ctk.CTkLabel(delete_window, text="Enter Restaurant Name to Delete",  
                     font=ctk.CTkFont(size=18, weight="bold")).pack(pady=20)  
 
        # שדה הקלדה של שם המסעדה  
        restaurant_name_entry = ctk.CTkEntry(delete_window, placeholder_text="Restaurant 
Name") 
        restaurant_name_entry.pack(pady=1 0)  
 
        def on_submit():  
            restaurant_name = restaurant_name_entry.get()  
 
            if not restaurant_name:  
                messagebox.showerror("Error", "Please enter the restaurant name.")  
                return  
 
            # שליחת בקשה לשרת למחיקת המסעדה  
            request_data = {  
                "action": "admin_delete_restaurant",  
                "restaurant_name": restaurant_name  
            }  
 
            self.client_comm.send(json.dumps(request_data).encode("utf -8"))  
            response = self.client_comm.receive()  
 
            if response:  
                try:  
                    response_data = json.loads(response)  
                    if response_data.get("status") == "success":  
                        cancelled_orders = response_data.get("cancelled_orders", [])  
 
                        def send_cancellation_emails():  
                            self.mail_sender = MailSender()  
                            for order in cancelled_orders:  
                                self.mail_sen der.send_cancel_restaurant_closed(  
                                    recipient_email=order["email"],  
                                    order=order  
                                )  
 
                        threading.Thread(target=send_cancellation_emails).start()  
 
                        messagebox.showinfo("Success", response_data["message"])  
                        delete_window.destroy()  
                    else:

                         messagebox.showerror("Error", response_data.get("message", "Failed to delete 
restaurant.")) 
                except json.JSONDecodeError:  
                    messagebox.showerror("Error", "Failed to decode server response.")  
            else:  
                messagebox.showerror("Error", "No response from server.")  
 
        ctk.CTkButton(delete_window, text="Delete Restaurant", command=on_submit,  
                      font=ctk.CTkFont(size=14),

    def close_connection(self): 
        if self.client_comm.socket:  
            try:  
                self.client_comm.send("exit")  
                response = self.client_comm.receive()  
                if response:  
                    print(response)  
            except Exception as e:  
                print(f"Error sending exit message: {e}")  
            finally:  
                try:  
                    if self.root.winfo_exists():  
                        messagebox.sho winfo("Connection Closed", "The connection to the server has been 
closed.") 
                except:  
                    pass  
                self.client_comm.close()  
                self.close_application()  
 
    def close_application(self): 
        # סוגר את כל החלונות בצורה נקייה  
        if self.root.winfo_exists():  
            self.root.quit()  
            self.root.destroy()  
 
 
    def run(self): 
        self.root.mainloop()
